## Capture Learnings

If you discovered a non-obvious pattern, pitfall, or architectural insight during
this session, log it for future sessions:

```bash
~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-learnings-log '{"skill":"design-review","type":"TYPE","key":"SHORT_KEY","insight":"DESCRIPTION","confidence":N,"source":"SOURCE","files":["path/to/relevant/file"]}'
```

**Types:** `pattern` (reusable approach), `pitfall` (what NOT to do), `preference`
(user stated), `architecture` (structural decision), `tool` (library/framework insight),
`operational` (project environment/CLI/workflow knowledge).

**Sources:** `observed` (you found this in the code), `user-stated` (user told you),
`inferred` (AI deduction), `cross-model` (both Claude and Codex agree).

**Confidence:** 1-10. Be honest. An observed pattern you verified in the code is 8-9.
An inference you're not sure about is 4-5. A user preference they explicitly stated is 10.

**files:** Include the specific file paths this learning references. This enables
staleness detection: if those files are later deleted, the learning can be flagged.

**Only log genuine discoveries.** Don't log obvious things. Don't log things the user
already knows. A good test: would this insight save time in a future session? If yes, log it.



## Additional Rules (design-review specific)

The audit rule "Never read source code" governs the Phases 1-6 audit only; the Phase 8 fix loop reads and edits source.

11. **Clean working tree required.** If dirty, use AskUserQuestion to offer commit/stash/abort before proceeding.
12. **One commit per fix.** Never bundle multiple design fixes into one commit.
13. **Only modify tests when generating regression tests in Phase 8e.5.** Never modify CI configuration. Never modify existing tests — only create new test files.
14. **Revert on regression.** If a fix makes things worse, `git revert HEAD` immediately.
15. **Self-regulate.** Follow the design-fix risk heuristic. When in doubt, stop and ask.
16. **CSS-first.** Prefer CSS/styling changes over structural component changes. CSS-only changes are safer and more reversible.
17. **DESIGN.md export.** You MAY write a DESIGN.md file if the user accepts the offer from Phase 2. Write it in the open DESIGN.md format (front matter tokens plus the canonical sections, the Phase 6 template in /design-consultation); an existing file keeps its persisted format choice, and this skill never offers a conversion.

[tool: Bash] B=/c/Users/itskm/.claude/skills/gstack/browse/dist/browse.exe; set -a; . <(grep -E '^(DEMO_USER_EMAIL|DEMO_USER_PASSWORD)=' /c/Users/itskm/car-dealership-kata/backend/.env | tr -d '\r'); set +a; timeout 60 "$B" viewport 1280x900 >/dev/null; timeout 60 "$B" goto http://localhost:5174/login >/dev/null; sleep 2; timeout 60 "$B" fill @e3 "$DEMO_USER_EMAIL" >/dev/null; timeout 60 "$B" fill @e4 "$DEMO_USER_PASSWORD" >/dev/null; timeout 60 "$B" click @e6 >/dev/null; sleep 4; timeout 90 "$B" goto http://localhost:5174/vehicles/21cf40da-155e-4da3-a786-3ab8d12f3d80 >/dev/null; sleep 8; cat > /tmp/ctx.js <<'EOF'
(async () => {
  const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
  const state = () => JSON.stringify({ svg: !!document.querySelector('svg[role=img]'), canvas: !!document.querySelector('canvas'), visible3d: !!document.querySelector('[data-tier]:not([aria-hidden=true])') });
  const out = { before: state() };
  const c = document.querySelector('canvas');
  const gl = c.getContext('webgl2') || c.getContext('webgl');
  const ext = gl.getExtension('WEBGL_lose_context');
  ext.loseContext();
  await wait(800);
  out.lost = state();
  ext.restoreContext();
  await wait(2500);
  out.restored = state();
  return JSON.stringify(out);
})()
EOF

[tool: Bash] B=/c/Users/itskm/.claude/skills/gstack/browse/dist/browse.exe; timeout 30 "$B" goto about:blank | tail -1; cd /c/Users/itskm/car-dealership-kata/frontend && npx vitest run 2>&1 | grep -E "×|Tests |Test Files"; cd .. && cat > .git/cove-msgs/159.txt <<'EOF'
fix(3d): recover from a lost WebGL context without hanging or losing 3D for good

Found by /qa in headless Chromium by forcing loseContext() then
restoreContext() on the detail page's canvas. Two defects:
1. On "restored" the scene remounted the whole Canvas (key={epoch}).
   React-three-fiber force-loses the old context when it unmounts, which
   fired the old canvas's "lost" listener again, so the viewer was told
   the context was lost after it had been restored and stayed on the SVG
   car for good.
2. Even after fixing 1, the tab stopped responding after a restore on the
   high tier (the browser listed it as "not responding"; a plain canvas
   lost and restored without the app does not hang). The postprocessing
   passes hold GPU objects from the dead context.

Now the Canvas is never remounted. Only the baked environment and the
contact shadow are keyed by epoch so they are redrawn after a restore;
the effect passes are removed while the context is lost and rebuilt after
it returns; and loss or restore events that arrive while the scene is
unmounting are ignored. Re-run in the browser: lost, restored, 3D car back
(SVG gone, one canvas), no hang. The existing CarViewer tests already
cover the lost/restored callbacks; the scene has no unit test (jsdom has
no WebGL). Suite passes; tsc and eslint clean.


Co-authored-by: Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF

[tool: Bash] cat >> docs/3d-decisions.md <<'EOF'
5. **No real shadow-map key light, no reflective floor.** `ContactShadows` on a transparent canvas over
   the page's own cove background gives the grounded look at a fraction of the cost.
   `MeshReflectorMaterial` (optional in the brief, "desktop-class only") was left out: it cannot be
   judged without real GPUs, and an unmeasured effect on the tier gate is the less conservative choice.
6. **Tone mapping is ACES Filmic** (the brief allows AgX or ACES); AgX desaturates the paints.
7. **The 3D car lives in a transparent canvas.** The page background shows through, so the "pale
   infinity-cove floor" is the page itself, not a mesh. Consequence: bloom light added over alpha 0 can
   leak past the canvas edge, so the bloom radius is kept small (0.3).
8. **Bloom is selective**, fed only by the head and tail light meshes, instead of a luminance threshold
   (which would have lit every bright reflection on the clearcoat). It needs scene lights by API; an
   empty `Object3D` satisfies that without adding light.
9. **Hover turn draws one car at a time** through one shared canvas (drei `View`), and only for a
   mouse pointer. Keyboard focus does not start it: the flat picture is the accessible one, and the
   detail page has the keyboard-operable viewer.
10. **The clay twin is a second render of the same car**, sliced by two clip planes at the sweep line,
    rather than swapping materials. Shared materials get clip planes only while the intro runs and
    lose them on finish or unmount.
11. **The page accent goes on the document root** (`usePageAccent`), animated with registered `@property`
    colours. Cards keep their own scoped accent. The existing contrast logic (`pickAccent`) is reused
    unchanged.
12. **Reduced motion:** SVG car, no camera or light motion, no ticking number; only the confirmation
    panel fades (300 ms, opacity only), by an override after the blanket reduced-motion rule.
13. **Hotspot notes use only facts the vehicle record holds** (body style, year, paint, stock, price).
14. **Verification of the scene is by real browser, not unit test.** jsdom has no WebGL. Pure helpers
    (geometry, tiers, poses, timelines, cache) are unit-tested; the scene was driven in headless
    Chromium (software GL) on the 3100/5174 dev servers. Frame rates and tier thresholds were not
    measured on real devices.
15. **QA used the local seeded accounts.** The demo buyer and admin credentials were read from
    `backend/.env` (never printed or committed) to sign in on the local dev app. One real purchase of a
    Mahindra Bolero Pik-Up was made to check the purchase moment: its dev-database stock went 9 to 8 and
    one order exists for the demo buyer. Restock it from the Admin page if you want the old number.
16. **A stale `.gstack/terminal-agent-pid.lock` (empty file, git-ignored) stopped the browse tool from
    starting.** No browse process was running, so the file was deleted. Chrome processes belonging to you
    were left alone.
17. **Measure before running tests.** A live WebGL tab in the headless browser starves the vitest
    workers (130 s and 35 timeouts instead of 28 s and none). The browser was parked on about:blank
    before every suite run.
18. **Bundle budgets were met without changes** (see docs/3d-report.md).
EOF
cat > docs/3d-report.md <<'EOF'

pushed, amended, squashed or rebased.

## Test results

- Frontend (vitest, jsdom, MSW): **27 files, 366 tests passed** at the last run; `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint .`
  and `prettier --check` clean. Before this work: 15 files, 171 tests.
- Backend untouched; its suite was not re-run.
- Not unit-testable here: the WebGL scene itself (jsdom has no GL). Covered by real-browser checks below.
- Real-browser checks (headless Chromium, software GL, dev servers on 3100/5174): 3D hero and detail render;
  eight body styles seen in at least one view (GR Supra, Bolero, Mini, Z4, Nexon EV, Emira in 3D; all
  silhouettes distinct); View select; hotspot buttons; clay-and-wireframe intro with Skip intro; purchase
  with confirmation panel; high-tier post-processing; one canvas on the showroom and one 3D car on
  hover; reduced motion shows the SVG only; WebGL context loss and restore (found and fixed a bug);
  axe-core (wcag2a/aa, 2.1 aa, 2.2 aa, best practice, colour contrast included) run in the real browser
  on landing, showroom, two detail pages and admin at 375 px and 1280 px: **no violations**; no
  horizontal overflow at 375 px; no console errors except the expected 401 of the signed-out session
  check and a "THREE.Clock deprecated" warning from react-three-fiber.

## Bundle sizes (gzip -9 of `npm run build` output)

| Part | gzip | Budget |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Initial JS (`index-*.js`) | 135,135 B (132 KiB) | under 200 KB |
| Initial CSS | 6,527 B | |
| 3D chunks, every 3D visitor (three + fiber + drei core 237,205; shared geometry/model 28,013; scene 9,808; View 1,968; fiber 2,177; hover 1,756) | 280,927 B (274 KiB) | under 700 KB |
| Post-processing chunk (high tier only) | 104,303 B | |
| 3D total with post-processing | 385,230 B (376 KiB) | under 700 KB |

The initial HTML references only the index chunk (no modulepreload), so no 3D code is fetched until a
viewer decides to use it. Both budgets pass; no fix was needed.

## What works

Landing page at "/" (signed out; signed-in visitors go to the showroom). Lazy `CarViewer` with the full
fallback chain (no WebGL, reduced motion, Save-Data, low-tier phone, error boundary, context loss and
restore), quality tiers with capped DPR, `frameloop="demand"`, off-screen and hidden-tab pause, shared
resource cache with disposal. Procedural cars for all eight body styles from `buildCarSpec`. Detail page
with specs, paint swatches, hotspots, purchase and keyboard/select viewing. Clay-to-render intro (once per
session, skippable). Page accent follows the paint (contrast-checked, animated). Purchase moment.
Post-processing on the high tier. One-canvas hover turn on showroom cards.

## What is unfinished or weak (honest list)

- **Looks:** noses are blunt, the truck bed is a plain dark panel, windscreens are slabs, wheels are all one
  design. Recognisable and distinct, not beautiful. No contact shadow is clearly visible in screenshots.
- **Thresholds and frame rates are unmeasured on real devices**; software GL only.
- The eased accent transition was verified by computed style, not by an intermediate sample.
- The scroll-scrubbed hero camera (GSAP/Lenis) from MASTER_PROMPT was not in the task list and not built.
  The 404 and auth-page ambient scenes and the admin form 3D preview (MASTER_PROMPT 10.3) were not built.
- README, test report and screenshots were not updated for the 3D work; `PROMPTS.md` was not touched.
- Earlier commit messages state some test counts that were slightly off (e.g. "325 tests" where the
  run said 334, "three failures" where two failed); the numbers in this report are from final runs.
- HoverTurn: keyboard focus does not start it (decision 9).

## Decisions

All in `docs/3d-decisions.md` (18 entries).

## How to view it

```
cd backend && PORT=3100 npm run dev          # API (reads backend/.env)
cd frontend && API_PROXY_TARGET=http://localhost:3100 npx vite --port 5174
```
Open http://localhost:5174/ signed out (hero intro plays once per browser session; clear sessionStorage
to see it again). Sign in, open the showroom (hover a card), then "View details" on any car.

## How to merge it

